more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011Cf2TyXqrsnBtJAAxdakxf","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01XwjuEMy4cnmwySJFkMkHBQ","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/qtclustering_claude-opus-5-cc-medium_mpi_r5/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp","content":"// QT Clustering Benchmark - MPI Distributed-Memory Version\n//\n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n//\n// Parallelization strategy (MPI):\n//   The outer loop of the algorithm is inherently sequential (each round\n//   commits exactly one cluster, which changes the input of the next round).\n//   The expensive part of every round is the independent evaluation of one\n//   candidate cluster per remaining unclustered seed point.  Those candidate\n//   clusters are distributed over the MPI ranks (cyclic seed ownership, which\n//   spreads the spatially correlated -- and therefore cost-correlated -- point\n//   groups evenly).  Each rank reduces its seeds to a local best candidate,\n//   an MPI_MAXLOC allreduce selects the global winner with exactly the same\n//   tie-breaking rule as the sequential code (largest cardinality, smallest\n//   seed index), and the owning rank broadcasts the winning member list so\n//   that every rank keeps a consistent global clustering state.\n//\n// Serial optimizations that preserve bit-identical results:\n//   * Incremental cluster growth: the maximum distance of every candidate to\n//     the current cluster is carried along and updated with a single distance\n//     evaluation per step instead of being recomputed from scratch.\n//   * Candidate pruning: a point can only ever join if it is within the\n//     threshold of the seed, and once its running maximum distance reaches the\n//     threshold it can never drop again, so it is removed from the work list.\n//   * Candidate-cluster memoization: a cached candidate cluster stays exactly\n//     valid as long as none of its members has been claimed by a committed\n//     cluster (the greedy choices only depend on the points that are actually\n//     picked), so only invalidated seeds are recomputed in later rounds.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <climits>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Per-rank scratch buffers for the candidate cluster construction.\nstruct GrowScratch {\n    std::vector<double> cx, cy, cmax;\n    std::vector<int> cidx;\n\n    void resize(int n) {\n        cx.resize(n);\n        cy.resize(n);\n        cmax.resize(n);\n        cidx.resize(n);\n    }\n};\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point.\n//\n// This reproduces the sequential reference exactly: in every step the still\n// unclustered point with the smallest maximum distance to all current members\n// is added, provided that distance stays strictly below the threshold; ties\n// are won by the smallest point index.  The maximum distances are maintained\n// incrementally (max() is order independent, so the values are bit-identical\n// to recomputing them over the whole member list).\nstatic int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                                    const double* __restrict px,\n                                    const double* __restrict py,\n                                    const unsigned char* __restrict clustered,\n                                    const double threshold,\n                                    const int point_count,\n                                    GrowScratch& s,\n                                    std::vector<int>& members) {\n    const double INF = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n\n    members.clear();\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n\n    double* __restrict cx = s.cx.data();\n    double* __restrict cy = s.cy.data();\n    double* __restrict cmax = s.cmax.data();\n    int* __restrict cidx = s.cidx.data();\n\n    // Initial candidate set: unclustered points within the threshold of the\n    // seed (the seed is a member, so anything farther can never be added).\n    const double sx = px[seed_point];\n    const double sy = py[seed_point];\n    int n = 0;\n    for (int i = 0; i < point_count; ++i) {\n        if (clustered[i] || i == seed_point) continue;\n        const double dx = px[i] - sx;\n        const double dy = py[i] - sy;\n        const double d = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n        if (d < threshold) {\n            cx[n] = px[i];\n            cy[n] = py[i];\n            cmax[n] = d;\n            cidx[n] = i;\n            ++n;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Pick the first point to add (smallest max distance, smallest index).\n    int best = -1;\n    double best_d = INF;\n    for (int k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n        if (cmax[k] < best_d) {\n            best_d = cmax[k];\n            best = k;\n        }\n    }\n\n    while (best >= 0) {\n        const double mx = cx[best];\n        const double my = cy[best];\n        members.push_back(cidx[best]);\n        // Force the freshly added member out of the candidate list below.\n        cmax[best] = INF;\n\n        // Update the running maximum distances (vectorizable).\n        for (int k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n            const double dx = cx[k] - mx;\n            const double dy = cy[k] - my;\n            const double d = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n            cmax[k] = cmax[k] > d ? cmax[k] : d;\n        }\n\n        // Compact out the candidates that can never join anymore and pick the\n        // next best one in a single pass.  Compaction preserves the ascending\n        // index order, so the tie-breaking stays identical to the reference.\n        int n2 = 0;\n        best = -1;\n        best_d = INF;\n        for (int k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n            const double md = cmax[k];\n            if (md >= threshold) continue;\n            cx[n2] = cx[k];\n            cy[n2] = cy[k];\n            cmax[n2] = md;\n            cidx[n2] = cidx[k];\n            if (md < best_d) {\n                best_d = md;\n                best = n2;\n            }\n            ++n2;\n        }\n        n = n2;\n    }\n\n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (MPI parallel over candidate seeds)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold,\n                                  const int rank,\n                                  const int nprocs) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n\n    // Structure-of-arrays copy of the coordinates for cache/SIMD friendliness.\n    std::vector<double> px(N), py(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        px[i] = points[i].x;\n        py[i] = points[i].y;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    // Cyclic seed ownership: rank r owns seeds r, r+nprocs, r+2*nprocs, ...\n    const int owned = (N - rank + nprocs - 1) / nprocs;\n    std::vector<std::vector<int>> cache(owned > 0 ? owned : 0);\n    std::vector<unsigned char> cache_valid(owned > 0 ? owned : 0, 0);\n\n    GrowScratch scratch;\n    scratch.resize(N);\n\n    std::vector<int> recv_members;\n    recv_members.reserve(N);\n\n    int remaining = N;\n    while (remaining > 0) {\n        struct { int card; int seed; } local, global;\n        local.card = -1;\n        local.seed = INT_MAX;\n\n        for (int slot = 0; slot < owned; ++slot) {\n            const int seed = rank + slot * nprocs;\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n\n            // A cached candidate cluster is still exactly valid as long as\n            // none of its members has been claimed by a committed cluster.\n            if (cache_valid[slot]) {\n                for (const int m : cache[slot]) {\n                    if (clustered[m]) {\n                        cache_valid[slot] = 0;\n                        break;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            if (!cache_valid[slot]) {\n                generateCandidateCluster(seed, px.data(), py.data(),\n                                         clustered.data(), threshold, N,\n                                         scratch, cache[slot]);\n                cache_valid[slot] = 1;\n            }\n\n            const int card = static_cast<int>(cache[slot].size());\n            if (card > local.card) {\n                local.card = card;\n                local.seed = seed;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Largest cardinality wins; MPI_MAXLOC breaks ties by the smallest\n        // seed index, exactly like the sequential scan in ascending order.\n        MPI_Allreduce(&local, &global, 1, MPI_2INT, MPI_MAXLOC, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        if (global.card <= 0) break;\n\n        const int owner = global.seed % nprocs;\n        recv_members.resize(global.card);\n        if (rank == owner) {\n            const int slot = (global.seed - rank) / nprocs;\n            std::copy(cache[slot].begin(), cache[slot].end(), recv_members.begin());\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(recv_members.data(), global.card, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = global.seed;\n        cluster.members = recv_members;\n        for (const int m : recv_members) {\n            clustered[m] = 1;\n        }\n        remaining -= global.card;\n        clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n    }\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n\n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n\n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]],\n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\",\n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n\n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\",\n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0, nprocs = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nprocs);\n\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\",\n                   num_points, threshold);\n        }\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"QT Clustering Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of points: %d\\n\", num_points);\n        printf(\"Distance threshold: %.2f\\n\", threshold);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // Generate synthetic data (deterministic; replicated on every rank)\n    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);\n    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);\n\n    // Perform QT clustering\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold, rank, nprocs);\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(\n        cluster_end - cluster_start);\n\n    int exit_code = 0;\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Clustering time: %ld ms\\n\", cluster_time.count());\n        printf(\"Clusters found: %zu\\n\", clusters.size());\n\n        // Calculate statistics and performance metrics\n        int total_clustered = 0;\n        int max_cluster_size = 0;\n\n        for (size_t i = 0; Those candidate\n//   clusters are distributed over the MPI ranks (cyclic seed ownership, which\n//   spreads the spatially correlated -- and therefore cost-correlated -- point\n//   groups evenly).  Each rank reduces its seeds to a local best candidate,\n//   an MPI_MAXLOC allreduce selects the global winner with exactly the same\n//   tie-breaking rule as the sequential code (largest cardinality, smallest\n//   seed index), and the owning rank broadcasts the winning member list so\n//   that every rank keeps a consistent global clustering state.\n//\n// Serial optimizations that preserve bit-identical results:\n//   * Incremental cluster growth: the maximum distance of every candidate to\n//     the current cluster is carried along and updated with a single distance\n//     evaluation per step instead of being recomputed from scratch.\n//   * Candidate pruning: a point can only ever join if it is within the\n//     threshold of the seed, and once its running maximum distance reaches the\n//     threshold it can never drop again, so it is removed from the work list.\n//   * Candidate-cluster memoization: a cached candidate cluster stays exactly\n//     valid as long as none of its members has been claimed by a committed\n//     cluster (the greedy choices only depend on the points that are actually\n//     picked), so only invalidated seeds are recomputed in later rounds.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <climits>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Per-rank scratch buffers for the candidate cluster construction.\nstruct GrowScratch {\n    std::vector<double> cx, cy, cmax;\n    std::vector<int> cidx;\n\n    void resize(int n) {\n        cx.resize(n);\n        cy.resize(n);\n        cmax.resize(n);\n        cidx.resize(n);\n    }\n};\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point.\n//\n// This reproduces the sequential reference exactly: in every step the still\n// unclustered point with the smallest maximum distance to all current members\n// is added, provided that distance stays strictly below the threshold; ties\n// are won by the smallest point index.  The maximum distances are maintained\n// incrementally (max() is order independent, so the values are bit-identical\n// to recomputing them over the whole member list).\nstatic int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                                    const double* __restrict px,\n                                    const double* __restrict py,\n                                    const unsigned char* __restrict clustered,\n                                    const double threshold,\n                                    const int point_count,\n                                    GrowScratch& s,\n                                    std::vector<int>& members) {\n    const double INF = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n\n    members.clear();\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n\n    double* __restrict cx = s.cx.data();\n    double* __restrict cy = s.cy.data();\n    double* __restrict cmax = s.cmax.data();\n    int* __restrict cidx = s.cidx.data();\n\n    // Initial candidate set: unclustered points within the threshold of the\n    // seed (the seed is a member, so anything farther can never be added).\n    const double sx = px[seed_point];\n    const double sy = py[seed_point];\n    int n = 0;\n    for (int i = 0; i < point_count; ++i) {\n        if (clustered[i] || i == seed_point) continue;\n        const double dx = px[i] - sx;\n        const double dy = py[i] - sy;\n        const double d = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n        if (d < threshold) {\n            cx[n] = px[i];\n            cy[n] = py[i];\n            cmax[n] = d;\n            cidx[n] = i;\n            ++n;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Pick the first point to add (smallest max distance, smallest index).\n    int best = -1;\n    double best_d = INF;\n    for (int k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n        if (cmax[k] < best_d) {\n            best_d = cmax[k];\n            best = k;\n        }\n    }\n\n    while (best >= 0) {\n        const double mx = cx[best];\n        const double my = cy[best];\n        members.push_back(cidx[best]);\n        // Force the freshly added member out of the candidate list below.\n        cmax[best] = INF;\n\n        // Update the running maximum distances (vectorizable).\n        for (int k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n            const double dx = cx[k] - mx;\n            const double dy = cy[k] - my;\n            const double d = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n            cmax[k] = cmax[k] > d ? cmax[k] : d;\n        }\n\n        // Compact out the candidates that can never join anymore and pick the\n        // next best one in a single pass.  Compaction preserves the ascending\n        // index order, so the tie-breaking stays identical to the reference.\n        int n2 = 0;\n        best = -1;\n        best_d = INF;\n        for (int k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n            const double md = cmax[k];\n            if (md >= threshold) continue;\n            cx[n2] = cx[k];\n            cy[n2] = cy[k];\n            cmax[n2] = md;\n            cidx[n2] = cidx[k];\n            if (md < best_d) {\n                best_d = md;\n                best = n2;\n            }\n            ++n2;\n        }\n        n = n2;\n    }\n\n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (MPI parallel over candidate seeds)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold,\n                                  const int rank,\n                                  const int nprocs) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n\n    // Structure-of-arrays copy of the coordinates for cache/SIMD friendliness.\n    std::vector<double> px(N), py(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        px[i] = points[i].x;\n        py[i] = points[i].y;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    // Cyclic seed ownership: rank r owns seeds r, r+nprocs, r+2*nprocs, ...\n    const int owned = (N - rank + nprocs - 1) / nprocs;\n    std::vector<std::vector<int>> cache(owned > 0 ? owned : 0);\n    std::vector<unsigned char> cache_valid(owned > 0 ? owned : 0, 0);\n\n    GrowScratch scratch;\n    scratch.resize(N);\n\n    std::vector<int> recv_members;\n    recv_members.reserve(N);\n\n    int remaining = N;\n    while (remaining > 0) {\n        struct { int card; int seed; } local, global;\n        local.card = -1;\n        local.seed = INT_MAX;\n\n        for (int slot = 0; slot < owned; ++slot) {\n            const int seed = rank + slot * nprocs;\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n\n            // A cached candidate cluster is still exactly valid as long as\n            // none of its members has been claimed by a committed cluster.\n            if (cache_valid[slot]) {\n                for (const int m : cache[slot]) {\n                    if (clustered[m]) {\n                        cache_valid[slot] = 0;\n                        break;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            if (!cache_valid[slot]) {\n                generateCandidateCluster(seed, px.data(), py.data(),\n                                         clustered.data(), threshold, N,\n                                         scratch, cache[slot]);\n                cache_valid[slot] = 1;\n            }\n\n            const int card = static_cast<int>(cache[slot].size());\n            if (card > local.card) {\n                local.card = card;\n                local.seed = seed;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Largest cardinality wins; MPI_MAXLOC breaks ties by the smallest\n        // seed index, exactly like the sequential scan in ascending order.\n        MPI_Allreduce(&local, &global, 1, MPI_2INT, MPI_MAXLOC, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        if (global.card <= 0) break;\n\n        const int owner = global.seed % nprocs;\n        recv_members.resize(global.card);\n        if (rank == owner) {\n            const int slot = (global.seed - rank) / nprocs;\n            std::copy(cache[slot].begin(), cache[slot].end(), recv_members.begin());\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(recv_members.data(), global.card, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = global.seed;\n        cluster.members = recv_members;\n        for (const int m : recv_members) {\n            clustered[m] = 1;\n        }\n        remaining -= global.card;\n        clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n    }\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n\n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n\n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0, nprocs = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nprocs);\n\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    \n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    // Add seed point\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n    \n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n    \n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\", \n               num_points, threshold);\n        return 1;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"QT Clustering Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of points: %d\\n\", num_points);\n    printf(\"Distance threshold: %.2f\\n\", threshold);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \n    printf(\"Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\\n\", \n           total_clustered, num_points, \n           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);\n    printf(\"Average cluster size: %.2f\\n\", {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011Cf2URbpx42QMiH4MwJL7g","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. The QT clustering benchmark in `qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp` is now unconditionally MPI-parallel.\n\n## Parallelization\n\nThe outer loop is inherently sequential (each round commits one cluster, changing the next round's input), so the parallelism is inside the round: one independent candidate cluster is grown per remaining unclustered seed.\n\n- **Cyclic seed ownership** (`seed % nprocs`) — points are generated in spatially correlated groups, so cyclic assignment spreads the cost-correlated groups evenly across ranks.\n- **`MPI_Allreduce` with `MPI_2INT`/`MPI_MAXLOC`** picks the global winner. MAXLOC breaks ties by smallest index, which is exactly the sequential rule (largest cardinality; first encountered in ascending seed order).\n- **`MPI_Bcast`** from the owning rank distributes the winning member list, so every rank keeps consistent global `clustered` state. Two collectives per committed cluster.\n- Data generation is replicated (deterministic, O(N)); only rank 0 prints, and the validation exit code is broadcast so all ranks agree.\n\n## Serial optimizations (all bit-identical)\n\n1. **Incremental growth** — each candidate carries a running max-distance to the cluster, updated with one distance evaluation per step instead of rescanning all members. `max` is order-independent, so values are identical.\n2. **Pruning** — a point can only join if it is within the threshold of the seed, and the running max never decreases, so exhausted candidates are compacted out (compaction preserves ascending index order, keeping tie-breaks identical).\n3. **Memoization** — a cached candidate cluster stays exactly valid until one of its *members* is claimed, since the greedy choices depend only on the points actually picked. Only invalidated seeds are recomputed.\n4. SoA coordinates + `__restrict` for a vectorizable distance-update loop.\n\n## Results\n\nVerified bit-identical hashes against the original binary across `-n 800 -t 1.0`, `-n 1500 -t 3.0`, `-n 2000 -t 0.5`, `-n 1000 -t 2.0` at 1, 7, 16 and 32 ranks; validation passes.\n\n| n=30000, t=2.0 | 1 | 8 | 32 | 64 | 128 |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| time (ms) | 46730 | 6417 | 1911 | 1176 | 757 |\n| speedup | 1.0x | 7.3x | 24.5x | 39.7x | 61.7x |\n\nThe serial algorithmic work also dropped sharply on its own (n=1000 went from 7018 ms to 12 ms single-rank).\n\nOne note: I killed my own leftover background reference run of the original code at n=4000 — it had exceeded 15 minutes and the equivalence was already confirmed at four other parameter sets, so I did not wait for that particular data point."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":778,"cache_read_input_tokens":46433,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":778},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"775953dc-a527-4491-a5d5-12196ad2bef6","uuid":"17affb25-d3ba-4f8e-a044-a042da9fcf0a","timestamp":"2026-09-14T01:35:51.908Z","request_id":"req_011Cf2URbFTyDS1Sij9p74Df"}
